      FROM (

          SELECT c0."ChatMessageId", c0."DocumentMetadataId", d."Id", d."ContentType", d."DocumentType", d."FileName", d."FileSize", d."IsProcessed", d."ManagedFileId", d."OriginalFileName", d."ProcessedAt", d."ProcessingError", d."StorageKey", d."TotalPages", d."UploadedAt"
          FROM "ChatMessageAttachments" AS c0
          INNER JOIN "DocumentMetadatas" AS d ON c0."DocumentMetadataId" = d."Id"
      ) AS s ON c."Id" = s."ChatMessageId"
      WHERE c."ConversationId" = @id
      ORDER BY c."Timestamp", c."Id", s."ChatMessageId", s."DocumentMetadataId"

      SELECT c."Id", c."Content", c."ConversationId", c."Model", c."Role", c."Timestamp", c."TokenCount", s."ChatMessageId", s."DocumentMetadataId", s."Id", s."ContentType", s."DocumentType", s."FileName", s."FileSize", s."IsProcessed", s."ManagedFileId", s."OriginalFileName", s."ProcessedAt", s."ProcessingError", s."StorageKey", s."TotalPages", s."UploadedAt"
      FROM "ChatMessages" AS c
      LEFT JOIN (
          SELECT c0."ChatMessageId", c0."DocumentMetadataId", d."Id", d."ContentType", d."DocumentType", d."FileName", d."FileSize", d."IsProcessed", d."ManagedFileId", d."OriginalFileName", d."ProcessedAt", d."ProcessingError", d."StorageKey", d."TotalPages", d."UploadedAt"
          FROM "ChatMessageAttachments" AS c0
          INNER JOIN "DocumentMetadatas" AS d ON c0."DocumentMetadataId" = d."Id"
      ) AS s ON c."Id" = s."ChatMessageId"

      ORDER BY c."Timestamp", c."Id", s."ChatMessageId", s."DocumentMetadataId"

      SELECT c."Id", c."Content", c."ConversationId", c."Model", c."Role", c."Timestamp", c."TokenCount", s."ChatMessageId", s."DocumentMetadataId", s."Id", s."ContentType", s."DocumentType", s."FileName", s."FileSize", s."IsProcessed", s."ManagedFileId", s."OriginalFileName", s."ProcessedAt", s."ProcessingError", s."StorageKey", s."TotalPages", s."UploadedAt"
      FROM "ChatMessages" AS c
      LEFT JOIN (
          SELECT c0."ChatMessageId", c0."DocumentMetadataId", d."Id", d."ContentType", d."DocumentType", d."FileName", d."FileSize", d."IsProcessed", d."ManagedFileId", d."OriginalFileName", d."ProcessedAt", d."ProcessingError", d."StorageKey", d."TotalPages", d."UploadedAt"

      SELECT c."Id", c."Content", c."ConversationId", c."Model", c."Role", c."Timestamp", c."TokenCount", s."ChatMessageId", s."DocumentMetadataId", s."Id", s."ContentType", s."DocumentType", s."FileName", s."FileSize", s."IsProcessed", s."ManagedFileId", s."OriginalFileName", s."ProcessedAt", s."ProcessingError", s."StorageKey", s."TotalPages", s."UploadedAt"
      FROM "ChatMessages" AS c
      LEFT JOIN (
          SELECT c0."ChatMessageId", c0."DocumentMetadataId", d."Id", d."ContentType", d."DocumentType", d."FileName", d."FileSize", d."IsProcessed", d."ManagedFileId", d."OriginalFileName", d."ProcessedAt", d."ProcessingError", d."StorageKey", d."TotalPages", d."UploadedAt"
          FROM "ChatMessageAttachments" AS c0
          INNER JOIN "DocumentMetadatas" AS d ON c0."DocumentMetadataId" = d."Id"
      ) AS s ON c."Id" = s."ChatMessageId"
      WHERE c."ConversationId" = @id
      ORDER BY c."Timestamp", c."Id", s."ChatMessageId", s."DocumentMetadataId"
